---
title: Test snapshot, 2026-11-20
description: Read-only ledger of all 10,593 unit, browser and integration tests, with delete, fix, consolidate and move-layer leads and a gap table.
---

- A snapshot is a read-only ledger of every test, marked by a worker; nothing in the repository was edited.
- The marks come from Haiku 5.5 high workers, so each flagged row is a lead; a later audit confirms a row before acting on it, and nothing is ever deleted from this file alone.
- Headline: 10,593 tests, 9,903 retained (320 of them only by a keep-guard), 686 flagged: 339 fix, 179 consolidate, 116 delete candidates, 52 move layer. The workers' test totals add to 10,593 while their marks add to 10,589: 4 tests were counted but not marked, a worker miscount to settle in the next snapshot.
- Layers: unit 5,600, browser/component 3,474, integration 1,519, e2e 0 (not in scope).

## Failures that matter (assumed)

1. A recording is lost or corrupted (capture, encode, save, crash recovery).
2. An upload or share fails silently, or exposes a private recording.
3. Auth, session or entitlement is wrong (free user gets paid features, paid user loses them, billing).
4. Secrets or personal data leak (logs, env, redaction).
5. A migration damages stored data (database or persisted settings).
6. Native permission, shortcut or input-hook failures.
7. A third-party contract breaks (payment webhooks, email, object storage API).
8. Wrong defaults or settings after an update; locale fallback.

## Baseline

`bun run test` at the repo root on main 60b07f90d: 12 turbo tasks successful, exit 0.

Scope: 814 test files, 167,777 lines, unit, browser and integration suites. The 12 Playwright `*.e2e.ts` files under `apps/kaipu-record/e2e` were NOT in scope, which is why the e2e layer count is 0.

## Delete candidates (D) (116 rows)

Leads only. Group "Test-only seam" is the riskiest: it assumes production never calls the helper, and one row in it says the behaviour is not asserted on the product path.

### Race or ordering fix (18)

| Test                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Evidence                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `apps/kaipu-record/src/main/library/library-vault.rename.test.ts::LibraryVault.renameItem > an acquisition queued behind a rename is refused if its window went away meanwhile`                                   | `vi.waitFor(() => expect(release).toBeTypeOf("function"))` polls for the sleep to start. Looks like polling; the race it guards is the one the name states. |
| `apps/kaipu-record/src/main/library/vault-activity.test.ts::VaultActivity — the admission contract > waits for short operations in flight, and sets the flag in the step their last lease ends`                   | `flush()` is `setTimeout(0)` followed by `expect(entered).toBe(false)`. A yield, not a time wait; kept for the admission ordering.                          |
| `apps/kaipu-record/src/main/recording/native/helper-clock.test.ts::reads the helper clock from the same continuous clock as Node hrtime`                                                                          | String check on Swift source. | `apps/kaipu-record/src/main/recording/native/helper-clock.test.ts::never reads the sleep-excluding host time clock`                                                                                               | Asserts two forbidden symbols are absent from non-comment Swift lines. Same limit: a slept Mac is needed to reproduce the bug.                              |
| `apps/kaipu-record/src/main/workspaces/move-item.test.ts::the item lock > waits for a writer in flight; a write queued behind the move finds the item gone`                                                       | Sleeps 20 ms (L350) before checking the queued move. Guard: a late save must find the item gone. Await the queued lock instead.                             |
| `apps/kaipu-record/src/main/workspaces/switch-transaction.test.ts::switchWorkspace > waits for a call already running in the old workspace before it commits`                                                     | Sleeps 0 ms (L126) before asserting no persist yet. Guard: the commit must wait for the running call. Await a "started" signal instead.                     |
| `apps/kaipu-record/src/main/workspaces/switch-transaction.test.ts::switchWorkspace > refuses an unknown id, a read-only registry, and a second switch while one runs`                                             | Sleeps 0 ms (L178) so the first switch reaches prepare. Guard: a second switch is refused while one runs. Await a "started" signal instead.                 |
| `apps/kaipu-record/src/main/workspaces/relocate.test.ts::relocateWorkspace, the active workspace > does not overlap another transaction`                                                                          | Sleeps 10 ms (L239) so the first transaction starts. Guard: a second transaction is refused. Await a "started" signal instead.                              |
| `apps/kaipu-record/src/main/workspaces/switch-transaction.test.ts::switchWorkspace > runs the transaction in order: probe, prepare windows, drain, persist, drop captures, bump`                                  | Exact log (L47-53). | `apps/kaipu-record/src/main/workspaces/relocate.test.ts::relocateWorkspace, the active workspace > runs the switch transaction: prepare windows, drain, persist, drop captures, bump`                             | Exact log `prepare, drop captures, commit` (L146). | `apps/kaipu-record/src/renderer/src/features/library/hooks/use-missing-recording-recovery.test.ts::useMissingRecordingRecovery > re-lists once when the recording is missing, keeping the loader up`              | Advances fake timers 500 ms; asserts one refresh and loader shown. The retry delay is the behaviour.                                                        |
| `apps/kaipu-record/src/renderer/src/features/screenshots/overlay/capture-overlay.test.tsx::CaptureOverlay > acks after a short timer when the hidden window never runs a frame, and only once`                    | Fake timers: no ack at `HIDDEN_ACK_FALLBACK_MS - 1`, one ack at the limit, none for a late frame.                                                           |
| `apps/kaipu-record/src/renderer/src/features/screenshots/overlay/capture-overlay.test.tsx::a spare (loaded ahead of its pick) > asks main for the state once it listens, so a state sent before that is not lost` | Asserts `onState` runs before `requestState`. | `apps/kaipu-record/src/renderer/src/features/screenshots/overlay/capture-overlay.test.tsx::FrameBox (chrome and keys) > tells main the confirmed frame is being saved before its own settings write (NIW2-878)`   | Asserts `reportFrame` (`confirming: true`) runs before `updateSettings`. | `apps/kaipu-record/src/renderer/src/features/video-editor/use-serialized-commit.test.tsx::useSerializedCommit > a task that armed a render wait holds the next one until the owner renders`                       | `expect(order).toEqual(["first"])` after a 20 ms sleep. ## Gap hunt against the failure list

First pass, not proof: built from the ledger rows plus a grep of test names. A "covered" verdict means an owner test with a matching name exists, not that its assertions were reviewed.

| Failure                                                           | Owner tests (examples)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Verdict                                                                              |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 1. Recording lost or corrupted                                    | `apps/kaipu-record/src/main/recording/stranded-recordings.test.ts` (recovers old staged parts, never deletes a recovery), `recording-writer.test.ts`, `recording-hub.test.ts`, `library/vault-reconcile.fix.test.ts`, `use-missing-recording-recovery.test.ts`                                                                         | covered                                                                              |
| 2. Upload or share fails silently, or exposes a private recording | `packages/application/src/cloud/cloud.test.ts` (idempotent intent, tombstone, user B cannot reuse user A's intent, sweep/cancel races), `apps/server-hono/src/contract/upload-abuse.contract.test.ts`. No test names a share link or visibility rule: no share route test found in server-hono or application                          | weak (upload covered; share and privacy exposure gap)                                |
| 3. Auth, session or entitlement wrong                             | `packages/application/src/entitlements/entitlements.test.ts`, `manual-plan-grant.test.ts`, `apps/kaipu-record/src/shared/entitlements.test.ts`, `main/infrastructure/auth-store.test.ts`, `server-hono/src/lib/desktop-auth.flow.test.ts`, `account-linking*.test.ts`, `auth-contract.test.ts`. `packages/infra-auth` has no test file | weak (entitlement and session flows covered; billing and infra-auth config untested) |

Migration damages stored data                                  | `main/services/settings.service.test.ts`, `main/infrastructure/settings-store.test.ts`, `main/workspaces/registry*.test.ts`; storage-format and migration guards in the R\* rows. ## Comparison with the previous snapshot

No previous snapshot; this is the baseline. Next snapshot compares rows on file plus full test name (see the skill's Modes).

## How this was produced

53 shards of about 3,500 test lines, one Haiku 5.5 high worker each, cs-audit-tests 1.1.0 snapshot mode ([PR 136, cs-tack](https://github.com/niway-dev/cs-tack/pull/136)), merged by Sonnet 5.5. Group names were normalized from the workers' free-text pattern labels by keyword; the original evidence is kept per row.
